# B30-20, chunk one: the exchange voided the trades, the market ceased for eight days, and it could only restart by installing new rules

**Read 2026-08-31** against §4.12's three-part test, fixed the same day: a relied-upon
convention is withdrawn or violated on a date; **the market then stops, meaning the
price ceases to exist rather than merely moving**; and restarting requires installing a
convention. **The London Metal Exchange nickel episode meets all three.**

Source: John Heilbron, *Central Clearing and Trade Cancellation: The Case of LME
Nickel Contracts on March 8, 2022*, Office of Financial Research working paper 24-09,
2024-12-10.

## The timeline, to the minute

| time, GMT, 2022-03-08 | event |
|---|---|
| | price had reached **$50,300/mt on 2022-03-07** |
| 01:00 | exchange opens for Asian trading |
| **06:08** | **price of 3M nickel peaks above $101,000/mt** |
| 06:16 | **LME Clear freezes margin requirements** |
| **08:15** | **LME suspends trade in nickel** |
| **12:00** | **LME cancels all 08 March nickel trades**, those placed between 01:00 and 08:15 |
| | trade **only resumed on March 16** |

**Scale of the cancellation:** *"5,000-9,000 trades with an aggregate market value of
$3.9 billion-$12 billion"*, and *"The cancellation eliminated $1.3 billion of profit
and loss between parties"*.

## The three parts

**1. The convention violated is the deepest one in any market: a trade is a trade.**
Executed, matched, cleared trades were voided several hours after the fact, and 1.3
billion dollars of profit and loss between parties was erased. **Not adjusted.
Erased.**

**2. The market then ceased, for eight days.** Not thin, not volatile. **No price.**

**3. It restarted only by installing conventions it had not had.** Following the
episode the LME implemented **price movement limits, OTC position reporting
requirements, and the suspension of Asian trading hours**, the last later reversed in
March 2023.

## The part that matters most for this chain

**The $101,000 price was produced by the market's own mechanism working exactly as
designed.** No error, no fault, no failure of the matching engine. **It was rejected
because honouring it would have destroyed the participants.**

**So the price system carries an implicit condition that is invisible until it binds:**

> **A number that would bankrupt the clearing members is not a price.**

That condition is nowhere in any rulebook as a price rule, it is not in any pricing
theory, and **it was enforced retroactively, by the venue, against its own executed
trades, and later upheld by the courts.** The High Court and then the Court of Appeal
found for the LME.

**A price is therefore not simply what a buyer paid a seller. It is what a buyer paid a
seller and the venue subsequently allowed to stand.** Every price series this project
or anyone else uses is filtered by that condition, and the filter is only observable on
the days it fires.

## What this adds to the chain

**Theorem 1 says a global price exists only if the cycles close. This episode says
something upstream of that: whether a transacted number counts as a price at all is a
decision made by a party after the fact.** The trade graph itself is editable.

**And it is the sharpest available answer to whether conventions are load-bearing.**
Remove one, and the market does not reprice. **It stops.** And it comes back only when
a replacement is installed.

## Limits

1. **One episode, one venue, one metal.** §4.12 lists five further candidates and none
   has been read.
2. **The trade-value range is wide**, 3.9 to 12 billion dollars, and the paper gives it
   as a range rather than a figure.
3. **Whether the suspension means "no price existed" is this reading, not the paper's.**
   Heilbron does not make that statement. **What the paper establishes is the
   suspension and its length**, and the inference that the price ceased to exist is
   ordinary but is not sourced.
4. **The court outcome is from law-firm summaries here**, not from the judgments. The
   judgments should be read before the ratification point is used for anything.
5. **Nothing here reads whether the LME was right.** §4.12 forbids that and this chunk
   does not do it.

---

---

# B30-20, chunk two: a regulator legally compelled a company to keep publishing a number, because contracts depended on it existing

**Read 2026-08-31.** §4.12's candidate one. **Primary document: the FCA's own notice.**

## The instrument

Financial Conduct Authority, **Article 21(3) Benchmarks Regulation, Notice of First
Decision, 2021-09-10**, addressed to **ICE Benchmark Administration Limited**.

> *"the Financial Conduct Authority ... has decided to **compel** ICE Benchmark
> Administration Limited ... to continue publishing the following LIBOR versions"*

Six versions: 1, 3 and 6 month sterling LIBOR and the three equivalent yen tenors.
Period: **twelve months starting immediately after the final publication on 31 December
2021.**

The stated reason:

> *"the existence and the scale of these contracts referencing the 6 LIBOR Versions
> means that **widespread market disruption and a disorderly cessation would likely be
> caused**, if the Authority did not exercise its Article 21(3) power to compel IBA to
> continue publishing the 6 LIBOR Versions"*

## What that is

**The panel convention had collapsed.** Banks were no longer to submit, and without
submissions the number would simply have stopped existing on 2021-12-31.

**Rather than let it stop, the state ordered it manufactured.** The compelled versions
were **synthetic**: computed by formula from a term risk-free rate plus a fixed spread,
with **no panel submissions and no underlying transactions at all.**

> **The number's existence became a legal artifact. It continued because a regulator
> required a company to publish it, and for no other reason.**

**And the regulator's stated ground is not that the number was true, useful, or
informative. It is that contracts referenced it.**

## How it scores against §4.12's three parts, and it does not score three of three

| part | LME nickel, chunk one | LIBOR compulsion, here |
|---|---|---|
| 1. a relied-upon convention withdrawn or violated, dated | **yes**, trades voided 2022-03-08 | **yes**, panel submission ends 2021-12-31 |
| 2. the market then stops, the price ceases to exist | **yes, observed**, eight days | **not observed. Asserted counterfactually by the FCA** |
| 3. restart requires installing a convention | **yes**, price limits, position reporting, hours | **yes, pre-emptively**: a synthetic formula imposed by order |

**Part 2 is not satisfied as an observation here and that is recorded rather than
glossed.** What exists is the regulator's counterfactual, stated in a legal notice by
the party with the best information and the strongest reason to be accurate about it.

**So the two cases pair rather than repeat.** **The LME episode is the convention
withdrawn and the market observed to stop. The LIBOR episode is a state acting
specifically to prevent that, and saying in writing that it was preventing it.**

## What the pair establishes for the chain

1. **Conventions are load-bearing, and both a venue and a state behaved as though they
   knew it.** One let a convention break and had to shut the market; the other paid the
   price of compelling a private company rather than find out.
2. **A published price can outlive the thing it was a price of.** Synthetic LIBOR had
   no submissions and no transactions. **It was a number with a formula and a legal
   obligation behind it, and contracts settled against it.**
3. **This is the terminal-condition question 
   §1e discriminator seven, answered from the other end.** That section asked how a
   number can be wrong. Here is a number that cannot be wrong in any market sense at
   all, because there is no market behind it, **and it still functioned as a price
   because the contracts pointed at it.**


## Corroboration of the counterfactual, added 2026-08-31

**Limit 1 said the market stoppage here is the FCA's counterfactual and not an
observation. That is still true. But the counterfactual is not the FCA's alone.**

**A second sovereign reached the same judgment through an entirely separate process
and acted on it.** United States, **Adjustable Interest Rate (LIBOR) Act, enacted
2022-03-15**, as described in the Federal Reserve's implementing regulation:

> *"a uniform, nationwide solution for replacing references to USD LIBOR in **tough
> legacy contracts**"*

with contracts lacking adequate fallbacks having the reference

> *"**by operation of law**, be replaced by the Board-selected benchmark replacement"*

effective the first London banking day after 2023-06-30. And on the scale:

> *"LIBOR is also referenced in **trillions of dollars' worth** of business and consumer
> loans, bonds, securitizations, and nonfinancial corporate contracts"*

**So: a British regulator compelled a private company to keep manufacturing the number,
and the American legislature replaced the reference inside private contracts by
statute. Two states, two instruments, two processes, one judgment.**

### What this does and does not add

**It adds an independent actor.** An act of Congress is not a regulator's notice, and
the deliberation behind it was separate. **Two states agreeing is stronger than one.**

**It does not turn the counterfactual into an observation.** Both remain judgments
about what would have happened. **No jurisdiction ran the experiment.**

**And that refusal is itself the finding.** Faced with a number whose production
convention had collapsed, **not one authority was willing to find out what happens if
it simply stops.** Both intervened, at the cost of compelling a private company in one
case and of rewriting the terms of existing private contracts in the other.
**Rewriting private contracts by statute is not a small thing to do, and it was done to
avoid finding out.**

**Neither the Fed's regulation nor the FCA notice quantifies the tough-legacy stock.**
The Fed says only *"a significant number of existing contracts"*. **So the scale of the
thing being protected is still not measured by anyone**, which is the same absence
B30-18 found for the discounting compensation, in a different place.

**The EU is believed to have taken a comparable statutory-replacement power. Not
checked, not used.**

## Limits

1. **Part 2 is counterfactual**, above. **The strongest statement available is that the
   FCA believed cessation would cause widespread disruption**, not that it did.
2. **This notice covers sterling and yen.** The US dollar synthetic decision is a
   separate later action and is not read here.
3. **The synthetic methodology is described here from secondary knowledge**, term
   risk-free rate plus fixed spread. **The FCA's methodology confirmation was not
   fetched**, and the formula should be quoted from it before being used for anything.
4. **Nothing here reads whether compelling publication was the right decision.** §4.12
   forbids it.

---

---

# B30-20, chunk three: the counter-example. The convention went, nobody replaced it, and the market never came back

**Read 2026-08-31.** §4.12 registered auction rate securities as the case expected to
satisfy parts one and two and **fail part three**, and it was collected for that
reason. **Collecting a case chosen in advance to break the pattern is the point of it.**

Source: Adrian D'Silva, Haley Gregg and David Marshall, *Explaining the decline in the
auction rate securities market*, Federal Reserve Bank of Chicago policy discussion
paper 2008-7.

## The convention, and the regulator's own acknowledgement of it

The market reached **about $330 billion in 2007**. Rates were set by periodic auctions.
And the auctions were held up by the dealers:

> *"banks that specialized in running auctions would step in with their capital to
> prevent failures when bidding faltered"*

**The authorities knew.** In **May 2006** the SEC fined firms **$13 million** for

> *"interventions in auctions to prevent failed auctions without adequate disclosure"*

**Read what the charge was.** Not intervening. **Intervening without disclosing it.**
The regulator's response to discovering that the price was produced by dealer support
was to require the dealers to say so, and the market carried on for another twenty
months with the practice disclosed and unchanged.

## The withdrawal, and the market

> *"By February 2008, fears of auction failure became self-fulfilling"*

Auctions failed across the market from February 2008. Holders could not sell.

**And it did not come back.** Dealers marked the paper down **10 to 50 per cent**, and
by mid-August 2008 the average ARS rate was still about **80 basis points above LIBOR**,
which the authors call *"a very large spread by historical standards"*.

## Scored against §4.12, and it fails part three exactly as expected

| part | result |
|---|---|
| 1. relied-upon convention withdrawn, dated | **yes**, dealer support bids, February 2008 |
| 2. the market stops | **yes, observed** |
| 3. restart requires installing a convention | **no. Nothing was installed and it did not restart.** |

**What happened instead was not a new convention but a reallocation**: the securities
were pushed back onto the dealers through regulatory settlements, and the market was
wound down rather than reopened. **The settlement amounts are not collected here.**

## The pair this makes with LIBOR, which is the reason to have both

**Same structure, opposite decision by the authorities, opposite outcome.**

```
LIBOR   production convention collapses
        a state manufactures a replacement by legal order
        the number survives with no market behind it at all

ARS     production convention collapses
        nobody manufactures a replacement
        the number and the market both cease, permanently
```

**So the variable that decided whether the price continued to exist was not anything
about the underlying assets.** ARS were mostly municipal and student-loan paper that
kept paying. **What differed is whether an authority decided to keep the number
alive.**

> **A price continues to exist when someone with the power to require it decides that
> it should. That is the closest thing to a controlled comparison this class of
> evidence admits, and both arms of it are documented.**

## Limits

1. **The two cases differ in more than the intervention.** LIBOR was a reference rate
   inside contracts; ARS was an instrument with holders. **They are not the same object
   and the comparison is structural, not matched.**
2. **The settlement figures are not collected**, so what the dealers ultimately absorbed
   is unquantified here.
3. **The failure rate is not in the paper**, so "the market stopped" rests on the
   authors' description and on the price markdowns rather than on a count of failed
   auctions.
4. **Whether the SEC could have compelled continued support bids is not examined.** The
   contrast with LIBOR assumes the authorities had a choice, and that assumption is not
   tested.

## Where §4.12 stands after three cases

| case | part 1 | part 2 | part 3 |
|---|---|---|---|
| LME nickel 2022 | yes | **yes, observed** | yes |
| LIBOR 2021 to 2023 | yes | counterfactual, corroborated by a second state | yes, pre-emptive |
| **ARS 2008** | yes | **yes, observed** | **no** |

**Three cases, two of them with the stoppage observed, and one showing that installing
a replacement is a choice rather than an automatic consequence.** Candidates four and
five in §4.12, the 2019 repo episode and the 2010 flash crash, remain unread.

---

---

# B30-20, chunk four: the restart that only worked by removing the sellers

**Read 2026-08-31.** §4.12's three-part test sorts cases into restarted and not
restarted. **It has no room for the middle**, which is a restart attempted and only
partly achieved. **A fourth outcome is added to the test and one case is collected for
it.**

## The added outcome

```
part 3 as registered   restart requires installing a convention
part 3a, added         a restart was attempted and worked only by suspending the
                       conditions that make the resulting number a price
```

**3a is not a softer version of 3. It is a different thing.** In 3 the venue installs
rules and trading resumes on the same terms as before. **In 3a the venue produces
numbers again by removing participants or removing the ability to sell.**

## The case: Moscow Exchange, 2022

- **Closed 2022-02-25.** The longest closure since the fall of the Soviet Union.
- **Reopened 2022-03-24**, about a month later.

**The conditions of the reopening:**

| restriction | |
|---|---|
| instruments | **33 equities only** |
| session | **four hours**, 10:00 to 14:00 Moscow time |
| short selling | *"Short-selling on stocks will be banned"* |
| **foreign investors** | *"Foreign investors will not be able to sell stocks or OFZ ruble bonds until April 1"* |

**Read the fourth row against the first day's result.** The MOEX Russia Index was *"up
more than 10% around 20 minutes into trading"*, with Norilsk Nickel above +22 per cent,
Rosneft +20, Lukoil +16.

**A market reopened after a month of sanctions and a currency collapse, and it went
up.** It went up in a session in which **a large class of holders was legally forbidden
to sell**, short selling was prohibited, only thirty-three names traded, and the state
was a declared buyer.

> **Numbers were produced. Whether they were prices is exactly the question, and the
> venue's own rules are what make it a question: the mechanism that generated them had
> the sellers removed from it by law.**

## What this adds

**The three earlier cases all treat the market as something that either works or does
not.** This one shows the third state: **a venue can always produce numbers if it is
willing to change who is allowed to trade.** The output looks like a price series, it
is published, it is used for valuation and index construction, and **it is generated by
a rule about participation rather than by an exchange between willing parties.**

**That is upstream of everything this project measures.** B30-16 and B30-17 both read
published number series and asked how much information they carry. **This case says a
published series can be generated by a participation rule and still look like a
series.** No statistic run on the series alone would reveal it.

## Where §4.12 stands after four cases

| case | convention withdrawn | market stops | restart |
|---|---|---|---|
| LME nickel 2022 | yes | **yes, observed**, 8 days | **3**: price limits, position reporting, Asian hours suspended for a year |
| LIBOR 2021-2023 | yes | counterfactual, two states | **3**: a synthetic formula by legal order |
| ARS 2008 | yes | **yes, observed** | **none. Permanent.** |
| **MOEX 2022** | trading itself suspended | **yes, observed**, ~1 month | **3a: only by removing the sellers** |

**Note that the LME restart also belongs partly in 3a**: it resumed with price limits
and with Asian trading hours suspended until March 2023. **A restart with the venue's
own hours cut by a third is not a return to the prior terms**, and chunk one recorded
that as a rule change rather than as a partial restart. **That reading is corrected
here.**

## Limits

1. **One collected case for 3a, and it is from contemporaneous trade press**, not from
   an exchange notice or a research paper. **The MOEX and central bank notices should
   be fetched before this is used for anything load-bearing.**
2. **No quoted assessment that the resulting prices were meaningless.** The reading
   here rests on the rules and the first-day move, and **no commentator is being cited
   for it**, because none was found in the source.
3. **Whether the restrictions were the cause of the rise is not established.** Oil was
   also rising and the state fund was buying. **Three explanations, none separated.**
4. **Registered and not read**: the Athens Stock Exchange closed for five weeks in 2015
   and reopened under restrictions on domestic investors, which would be the mirror case
   with the constraint on buyers instead of sellers. **Unverified and named only as a
   candidate.**

---

---

# B30-20, chunk five: Athens 2015, and a mirror that is half sourced

**Read 2026-08-31.** §4.12 registered the Athens closure as the mirror of MOEX, with
the participation restriction on the other side of the book. **Half of that is sourced
and half is not, and the half that is not is the half the mirror depends on.**

## What is sourced

**Closed 2015-06-29. Reopened 2015-08-03. Five weeks.**

First day, from NPR's contemporaneous report: the index opened at **615 against a
previous 797.52**, about **minus 23 per cent**; recovered to 660 three hours in, about
minus 17; and **closed down 16.2 per cent**. Bank shares led, with Piraeus, Eurobank
and National Bank *"dropping by some 30 percent after trading began"*.

And the asymmetry exists, from Reuters quoting the securities regulator before the
reopening:

> *"The Greek stock market will reopen on Wednesday or Thursday after a one-month
> shutdown, **with restrictions on trading by local investors** for a short period of
> time"*

**So the restriction fell on domestic investors. That much is sourced.**

## What is not sourced, and it matters

**Which side of the book the restriction removed is not in any source fetched.** The
mirror reading needs Athens to have constrained **buying** the way MOEX constrained
**selling**. Under capital controls a Greek investor's cash was frozen in Greek banks,
so the practical effect would fall on purchases, **but that is an inference and not one
sentence of source supports it.**

**Recorded as unverified.** Four contemporaneous outlets were tried and two returned
403. **The primary instrument, the ministerial decision setting the reopening terms, was
not fetched, and it is what would settle this.**

## The pair, stated at the strength the sources allow

| | restriction | who it fell on | first day |
|---|---|---|---|
| **MOEX 2022-03-24** | **foreigners barred from selling**, short selling banned, 33 names, four hours | **sellers**, sourced | **+10 per cent in twenty minutes** |
| **Athens 2015-08-03** | *"restrictions on trading by local investors"* | **domestic investors**, sourced; **which side of the book, not sourced** | **minus 16.2 per cent at the close** |

**Two venues reopened after a month-long closure, each under an asymmetric
participation rule, and the first-day moves went in opposite directions by more than
twenty-six percentage points.** **Whether the direction tracks which side of the book
was removed is the question, and one of the two cells needed to answer it is
missing.**

**This is written down as a question rather than as a finding**, because the reading is
attractive and the evidence for half of it does not exist yet.

## What it does establish

**Nothing about the underlying assets changed on either reopening day.** Greek banks
were no more or less solvent on 3 August than on 2 August; Russian oil companies were
no more valuable at 10:20 Moscow time than at 10:00. **In both cases a number was
produced on the first day, and in both cases the venue had just decided by rule who
would be present to produce it.**

**That much needs no inference and it is the part of chunk four that survives here.**

## Limits

1. **The mechanism of the Athens restriction is unverified**, above. **The mirror is a
   hypothesis.**
2. **Both first-day moves have obvious alternative causes.** Greece had just accepted a
   third bailout programme with bank recapitalisation implied, which is a reason for
   bank shares to fall by themselves. Oil was rising in March 2022 and the Russian
   state fund was a declared buyer. **Neither move is attributed to the restriction
   here.**
3. **Two episodes is not a test.** §4.13 registers what a test would be.

# B30-20, chunk six: the missing cell, found in Greek. The restriction was on buyers, by name

**Read 2026-08-31.** Chunk five could not establish which side of the book Athens
restricted and recorded it as unverified after five English-language searches and four
fetch attempts. **Searching in Greek answered it in one query.**

## The operative provision

Καθημερινή, **2015-07-23**, Ανέστης Ντόκας, quoting the text prepared by the Bank of
Greece and the Hellenic Capital Market Commission **dated 20/7/2015** for the
ministerial decision, under the headline **«Μόνο με "νέο χρήμα" οι συναλλαγές όταν
επαναλειτουργήσει το Χρηματιστήριο»**, "transactions only with new money when the
exchange reopens":

> *"Εάν πρόκειται για **αγορά** των χρηματοπιστωτικών μέσων, τότε ο τραπεζικός
> λογαριασμός του δικαιούχου (**αγοραστή**) στο ίδρυμα, από τον οποίο πραγματοποιείται
> η μεταφορά, πρέπει να έχει πιστωθεί, μετά την έναρξη ισχύος της παρούσης, με **νέα
> κεφάλαια** τουλάχιστον ισόποσα με τις ανάγκες της εκκαθάρισης/διακανονισμού της
> συναλλαγής αγοράς"*

**"If it is a PURCHASE of financial instruments, then the bank account of the
beneficiary (the BUYER) from which the transfer is made must have been credited, after
this decision takes effect, with NEW FUNDS at least equal to the settlement needs of
the purchase transaction."**

**The provision names the buyer and applies only to purchases. Selling is not
constrained by it.**

"New funds", **νέα κεφάλαια**, are then enumerated: a remittance from abroad, the
proceeds of selling financial instruments, returned margin, distributions such as
coupons and dividends, credits from derivative positions, free credit balances already
in investment accounts, cash paid in, and a capped daily transfer from bank accounts.

**Money already sitting in a Greek bank account was not new funds.** That is the whole
mechanism: **the buyers' existing cash was made unusable and the sellers were left
free.**

Corroboration on the asymmetry, from two further Greek outlets reporting the ECB's
final recommendation:

> *"οι συναλλαγές των ξένων επενδυτών θα είναι **ελεύθερες**, ενώ οι Έλληνες επενδυτές
> **δεν θα έχουν τη δυνατότητα να προβαίνουν σε αγορές**"*

**"transactions by foreign investors will be free, while Greek investors will not have
the ability to make purchases."**

## The mirror, now sourced on both sides

| | which side the rule removed | first day |
|---|---|---|
| **Athens 2015-08-03** | **buyers**, named in the text as αγοραστή, required to use new funds | **minus 16.2 per cent** |
| **MOEX 2022-03-24** | **sellers**, foreigners barred from selling, short selling banned | **plus 10 per cent in twenty minutes** |

**Two venues reopened after a month-long closure. One made it hard to buy and fell
sixteen per cent. The other made it impossible for a large class to sell and rose ten.
The rule and the sign match on both.**

**Nothing about the underlying assets moved on either morning.**

## What this corrects, and it is a repeat offence

**§4.13a concluded that the primary text was not obtainable and used that to constrain
the arm's design.** It was obtainable. **The search had been run only in English.**

**That is failure mode 110 again**, concluding absence because the evidence was looked
for in the wrong form. **It has now happened twice in this chain**, once over a capture
date and a data series, and once here. **The rule that follows is added to the design
file: for any non-Anglophone jurisdiction, a negative on source availability may not be
recorded until the search has been run in the jurisdiction's own language.**

## Limits

1. **This is the draft text of 2015-07-20 as quoted by a newspaper**, prepared for a
   reopening then planned for 27 July. **The exchange reopened on 3 August and the
   final Government Gazette text has not been fetched.** The direction is corroborated
   by two further outlets reporting the ECB recommendation, so **the side of the book is
   established and the exact final wording is not.**
2. **Two episodes remain two episodes.** §4.13's sign test needs the other candidates,
   and this pair still cannot score in it, having been the pair that generated the
   hypothesis.
3. **Both first-day moves still have alternative causes**, per chunk five limit 2.
   **Nothing here attributes either move to the restriction.** What is established is
   that the rule and the sign line up in both cases and that the rules were opposite.

# B30-21, chunk one: the first excess return computed, and it goes against the prediction

```
CONTROL-CONTAMINATED:  2026-09-03, third pass
DO-NOT-QUOTE-THE-SIGN: the excess is positive at every window from one to twenty
                       sessions and decays monotonically, +3.69 at one session to
                       +0.30 at twenty. That decay is the signature of a control
                       that overshot while the local market was shut and then
                       converged. It is the competing explanation this arm
                       registered and had not tested, and it is now visible in the
                       data rather than argued.
FLOOR-CORRECTED:       2026-09-03, same day, third pass. The second pass took the
                       reopening session's own intraday range, about six points,
                       as the floor and concluded the reading sat under one
                       multiple of it. That was the wrong floor. It measures how
                       turbulent that day was, not how precise this statistic is.
                       The floor for a mean of N daily excesses is sd/sqrt(N); the
                       daily excess sd on the 42 common sessions after reopening
                       is 2.326 per cent. On that floor the original single-day
                       reading stands at 1.59 multiples, not under one.
SUPERSEDES-IN-PART:    the section "The excess, and it has the wrong sign" below,
                       its heading, and the second pass's floor argument. The
                       arithmetic in all of them is correct on its own inputs.
DATA:                  data/raw/athex_grek_2015.csv, 88 sessions, both legs on
                       unadjusted closes, ATHEX carries no value during the shut
                       weeks
```

> **CORRECTION 2026-09-03, and it is about resolution rather than about the data
> or the convention.**
>
> **Athens reopened on 2015-08-03 down more than 22 per cent in early trading and
> closed down about 16 per cent.** So the single session the local leg is read on
> **moved about six points within itself**, while the excess being read is `3.1`
> points. **The reading sits under the swing of its own reference day.**
>
> **A first pass this morning gave a different reason and it was wrong**, and the
> error is recorded rather than replaced silently. That pass said the defect was
> an unregistered choice between the close and the intraday low. **It is not.**
> Both legs are read close to close, that is consistent, and close to close is the
> standard convention; **switching the local leg to an intraday low while the
> control stays at a close would introduce the inconsistency, not remove it.**
>
> **The defect is that no floor was ever computed for this outcome variable.**
> Gate six (`D24`) asks how many multiples of the instrument's own measured floor
> a non-zero stands on, and it was never asked here. On this episode the answer is
> **less than one**. That is why the sign may not be quoted in either direction,
> and it is a statement about the instrument rather than about the world.
>
> Limit 3 below already records that the close used here is derived from a
> reported percentage rather than read off an index series. **That limit stands
> and is worth fixing, but it is not what decides this.**
>
> **What stands unchanged**: the outcome variable is computable, the control
> condition is satisfiable, and the competing explanation in point 3 below (a fund
> whose underlying market is closed becomes the price-discovery venue and
> overshoots) **is untouched by this correction and still has to be answered on
> either reading.**
>
> **What does not stand**: this block's heading, and any citation of the sign.


**Read 2026-08-31** under §4.13c's second design, registered the same day and **before
any control price was looked at.** This is the first computation of the new outcome
variable.

## The episode and the two series

**Athens 2015. Class B**, restriction on buyers, established from the operative text in
B30-20 chunk six.

**Closure window: 2015-06-26 last close, to 2015-08-03 reopening close.**

| | 2015-06-26 | 2015-08-03 | return |
|---|---|---|---|
| **ATHEX general index** | 797.52 | about 668.5 | **minus 16.2 per cent** |
| **GREK**, Global X MSCI Greece ETF, NYSE Arca, adjusted close | **29.3306** | **23.6778** | **minus 19.27 per cent** |

**GREK traded on every US session throughout the five weeks the Athens exchange was
shut**, which is §4.13c's fifth condition, and it is satisfied.

## The excess, and it has the wrong sign

```
local     -16.2%
control   -19.3%   (in US dollars, unadjusted)
excess    +3.1 percentage points
```

**§4.13c predicted a NEGATIVE excess for class B.** Athens restricted buyers, so the
domestic price should have been depressed relative to a market where nobody was
restricted. **It was not. The domestic index fell about three points LESS than the
offshore vehicle.**

## Before this is read as a refutation, two corrections it needs and one that may kill it

**1. Currency, and it is not optional.** GREK is priced in dollars and the ATHEX index
in euro. **The euro fell against the dollar over this window**, so part of GREK's
larger fall is the currency and not the equities. On approximate contemporaneous rates
the correction is on the order of two points, which would leave an excess of roughly
**plus one point instead of plus three.** **The FX series has not been pulled and this
number must be redone properly before it is used.**

**2. The control is not the same basket.** GREK tracks a large-cap MSCI Greece index;
the ATHEX general index is broader. **Composition difference is unquantified.**

**3. The one that may account for the whole result.** **An exchange-traded fund whose
underlying market is closed stops being a tracker and becomes the price-discovery
venue.** For five weeks GREK was the only place anyone could express a view on Greek
equities, and such vehicles are known to trade away from the value of their holdings
and to overshoot. **So a control that fell more than the domestic market on reopening
is exactly what an overshooting closed-market ETF would produce, with no need for any
restriction effect at all.**

**That is not a caveat, it is a competing explanation of the entire sign**, and
§4.13c's design did not anticipate it.

## What this chunk establishes

1. **The outcome variable is computable**, and the control condition is satisfiable for
   at least one episode.
2. **On the one episode computed, the class B prediction is not supported.** The excess
   is positive where negative was predicted.
3. **The result cannot yet be called a refutation**, because of the currency correction
   and, more seriously, because the control's own behaviour during a closure is a
   competing explanation that the design failed to consider.

## What the design now needs, registered rather than run

**A control that is not an ETF.** Individual **depositary receipts** of the same
companies, or the same companies' shares cross-listed on another exchange, have the
same closed-underlying problem but not the fund-premium mechanism. **Better still is a
control drawn from a different country entirely**, matched on exposure, which has no
closed underlying at all and therefore prices normally: **a regional index excluding
the closed country**, for instance.

**Both should be computed for Athens before this episode is scored**, and whichever is
adopted must be fixed for every later episode.

## Limits

1. **One episode. n equals one.**
2. **The FX correction is estimated and not computed.**
3. **The ATHEX reopening close is derived** from the prior close and the reported
   minus 16.2 per cent, not read from an index series.
4. **The competing explanation in point 3 above is not tested**, and until it is, this
   chunk reports a number rather than a finding.

---

# B30-22, chunk zero: the literature precondition ran, and it voids one of the three sub-designs before it cost anything

**Read 2026-08-31.** §4.14 made reading the border-effect literature a precondition
rather than a courtesy, on the ground that the known direction might be adverse.
**It ran, and it did two things: it voided sub-design C, and it handed the chain a
measured cycle defect.**

## What the literature says

Messner, Rumler and Strasser (2023), ECB Research Bulletin, on a **GfK household panel
for Austria and Germany, 2008 to 2018**, transaction-level prices of fast-moving
consumer goods, **within a 60 kilometre band along the border**:

> *"prices are about **13% higher on the Austrian side** of the border"*

and excluding products priced identically,

> *"cross-border price differences are centred around a **15-18% premium** on the
> Austrian side"*

with **about 14 per cent of products priced the same on both sides**. The authors'
explanation is that retailers *"maximise profits separately in each country, even
within the European Union."*

## Read the conditions

**One currency. One single market. No tariffs. No border controls. Sixty kilometres.
Identical products. And a persistent thirteen to eighteen per cent difference with a
sign.**

**That is a cycle that does not close, measured, in the place where the standard
construction has the fewest excuses left.** Germany to Austria and back is a
two-edge cycle on identical goods, and it sums to 13 to 18 per cent, for ten years.

## What it does to sub-design C, and this is why the precondition existed

**§4.14's sub-design C proposed the euro as the merge experiment: cycles closed on a
date, so dispersion should collapse. It did not collapse. Sub-design C as written is
void.**

**But the reason it is void is the framework's own distinction, and that has to be
stated carefully because it is post hoc.**

```
the euro unified the UNIT
it did not create an arbitrage cycle
a shopper in Salzburg does not arbitrage groceries to Freilassing at scale
so the trade graph's connectivity for these goods was never changed
```

**Theorem 7 says the surviving defect is set by what the traders can reach, not by what
units the numbers are quoted in.** So a currency union should leave the defect where it
was. **It did.**

**This is a fit, not a prediction.** The chain did not write this down before reading
the result. **What it does establish is that sub-design C was testing the wrong thing:
a coordinate merge, not a graph merge.** And that is the same distinction B30-18 found
in finance, where changing the discounting convention moved valuations and moved
nothing real.

**A real merge experiment has to remove a barrier to arbitrage, not a barrier to
quotation.** Registered as the correction.

## And it hands over a carrier

**The Austria-Germany border band is a measured cycle defect on identical goods**, with
a household panel behind it, ten years long, one currency, and a sign. **That is what
B29 and B30 have been looking for on a real carrier and it already exists in a
published paper.**

**It also carries the fraction that agreed**: about 14 per cent of products priced
identically. **§1a's anchor reading would want to know what distinguishes that 14 per
cent**, and the paper's own answer, separate profit maximisation by country, is a
competing explanation for the other 86 that has nothing to do with copying.

## Limits

1. **Read from the ECB's research bulletin summary, not the underlying paper.** The
   figures and the two quoted phrases are the bulletin's. **The paper itself should be
   read before any of this is built on.**
2. **Fast-moving consumer goods only**, and a 60 kilometre band. **Not general.**
3. **The framework's fit to the null result is post hoc**, above, and is labelled as
   such rather than counted.
4. **The competing explanation is the authors' own and it is good.** Separate
   country-level profit maximisation produces cross-border dispersion without any
   coordination story. **Nothing here separates the two.**

---

---

# B30-22, chunk one: the merge experiment is already run, on a better carrier, and it says the value was attached to the holder

**Read 2026-08-31.** §4.14 proposed a merge sub-design and chunk zero voided the euro
version of it. **The right version already exists as B32, run in a separate session, and B32's own
design and results are the authority for everything below**; **this chunk does not
re-derive anything and adds only what the B30 line takes from it.**

## The carrier

**German monetary, economic and social union, 1990-07-01.** Treaty signed
**1990-05-18**, **Annex I Article 6** fixes the conversion, primary text read by B32:

| holder | rate |
|---|---|
| natural persons resident in the GDR, within a per-person allowance by **birth date**: born after 1976-07-01 up to **2,000 M**; 1931-07-02 to 1976-07-01 up to **4,000 M**; before 1931-07-02 up to **6,000 M** | **1 : 1** |
| the same persons' balances **above** that allowance, **and all legal persons** | **2 : 1** |
| non-residents' balances **existing at 1989-12-31** | **2 : 1** |
| non-residents' balances **formed after 1989-12-31** | **3 : 1** |

And the allowance is **per person, not per account**:
*"Der Antrag kann nur einmalig bei einem Geldinstitut gestellt werden."*

## What the B30 line takes from it, in one line

> **The same mark, in the same bank, on the same day, was worth one, one half, or one
> third of a Deutsche Mark, depending on the birth year and the address of whoever held
> it.**

**A price is a property of the claim in the standard construction. Here the number was
assigned by holder identity, by decree, at the moment two systems joined.** That is the
strongest form of this chain's claim that the number is not a property of the thing,
and it is a treaty article rather than an inference.

## The parties then moved the claims to the holders with the better rate, and it was observed

B32 §R8b, from the primary record:

> *"1990 年五月与六月出现 zahlreiche Neueröffnungen von Konten, 儿童储蓄账户明显增加,
> 家庭把已有余额分散出去"*

**Numerous new account openings in May and June 1990, a marked rise in children's
savings accounts, households dispersing existing balances**, particularly into the
under-14 band that converted 2,000 M at par.

**The window has hard edges**: application forms available **1990-06-05**, returnable
only between **06-11 and 07-06**. **The account-opening wave sits before and inside
it.**

**This is a direct observation of an arbitrage on holder identity, not an inference.**

## And the drafters saw it coming, which is the part this chain wants

Annex I Article 6, fourth paragraph, **four words**:

> **"Umgehungsgeschäfte sind nichtig."** Circumvention transactions are void.

**A rule declaring circumvention void is an admission by its own author that the value
was assigned rather than found.** You cannot circumvent a fact about the world. **You
can only circumvent a rule.** The clause exists because the drafters knew the number
was attached to the holder and that holders are movable.

## What it does to §4.14

**Sub-design C is closed by B32 rather than run here.** And the closure is better than
the design asked for:

| §4.14 sub-design C asked for | B32 delivered |
|---|---|
| cycles closed on a date | two systems merged on a date, treaty text in hand |
| dispersion collapses | **the state assigned three different values to identical claims**, which is a stronger object than dispersion collapsing |
| unanticipated | the conversion rates were set 1990-05-18 for 1990-07-01, and the arbitrage window was six weeks |

**Sub-designs A, redenomination, and B, currency-area split, remain open and are the
B30 line's own work.** Czechoslovakia 1993 is still the split case and its two
preconditions, surprise and physical continuity, are unchecked.

## Two things B32 has that this chain should not duplicate

1. **An out-of-sample scoring.** B32 §R7a records that the manuscript's section 15
   already contained this instrument, from 1949 Wuhan: 500 yuan per person at par, the
   rest at 1:5. **Same shape, forty-one years apart, two countries**, and the 1990
   German conversion appears nowhere in the manuscript, verified by word count.
   **That is a genuine out-of-sample hit and it belongs to B32, not here.**
2. **An identity verified by two independent paths.** 0.33506 derived from published
   totals against 0.3425 from the savings banks' own books, 0.74 percentage points
   apart, **with no common input.**

## Limits

1. **Everything here is read from B32's result file, not re-derived.** **B32 is the
   authority and any correction there governs this chunk.**
2. **B32 marks parts of its own §R7b as withdrawn.** The withdrawn half concerned the
   allowance being filled to 100 per cent; **the arithmetic and the direct observation
   of the moves are retained by B32 and only those are used here.**
3. **The B30 line adds one reading of its own**, the circumvention clause as an
   admission. **That is an argument, not a measurement, and B32 does not make it.**

## B30-22 第二块：捷克斯洛伐克 1993-02-08，两条前置条件的查证结果

```
AUTHORITY:  §4.14 子设计乙的前置条件查证。不是结果，是「这个载体能不能上马」的裁决。
DATE:       2026-08-31
STATUS:     前置条件一不通过，条件二通过。子设计乙由事件研究降为描述臂。
            同时由此给 §4.14 加一条载体先验剔除规则。
```

### 一、要查的是什么

§4.14 在任何一次运行之前注册了两条前置条件，原文：

> 确认「意外」。每一例都要拿到当时的证据说明它没被预期（提前通知的天数、
> 事前的远期或黑市汇率有没有提前反应）。捷克那次只提前几天通知，这一点要用一级来源坐实。

> 确认实体连续。跨过那个日期，产能、库存、运输没有同时被战争或封锁打断。
> 打断了的例子出局，否则第二条力量就没了。

**下面两条分开裁。**

---

### 二、时间线，带日期

| 日期 | 事件 | 出处 |
|---|---|---|
| 1992-07 末 | 捷克财政部订购一亿六千万张**印花**，为分币做准备 | Novinky 2013 |
| 1992 夏 | 「Legislativní práce na měnové odluce začaly v létě roku 1992」 | Novinky 2013 |
| 1992-08-26 | 克劳斯与梅恰尔在图根哈特别墅谈联邦解体安排 | Newstream 2023 |
| 1992 年末 | 两共和国签货币安排条约，**约定共同货币至少维持 1993 上半年** | McLean 2012 |
| 1993-01-01 | 联邦解体，**货币联盟保留** | IHS 摘要 |
| 1993-01 全月 | 「many Slovak firms and individuals transferred funds to Czech commercial banks **in expectation of Slovak devaluation**」 | McLean 2012 |
| **1993-01-15** | 捷克财长 Kočárník 公开否认：**「V žádném případě nedojde k měnové odluce zítra」**；梅恰尔同日称两国总理之间**尚不存在**任何分币协议 | ČTK 存档 |
| 1993-02-02 | 两国**议定**拆分 | McLean 2012 |
| **1993-02-03** | **公开宣布**。「V mezidobí mezi ohlášením odluky 3. února a její realizací 8. února byl **zastaven veškerý platební styk**」 | 捷克国家银行行史 |
| 1993-02-04 至 02-07 | 资金流动冻结 | McLean 2012 |
| **1993-02-08** | 新货币生效，未贴印花的百元及以上钞票在**一夜之间**「staly jen papírky」 | Aktuálně 2018 |
| 1993-02-08 至 02-12 | 换钞窗口，**15 岁以上每人 4000 克朗**，超出部分只能进账户或邮政汇票 | 捷克国家银行行史；Novinky 2013 |
| 1993-07 | 斯洛伐克央行**行政决定**，把斯洛伐克克朗对捷克克朗从 1:1 的起始平价**贬值 10%** | McLean 2012 |
| 1993-08-31 | 贴花联邦钞票失效 | Novinky 2013 |
| 1994-05-31 | 银行柜台停止兑换旧币 | Novinky 2013 |

---

### 三、前置条件一（意外）：**不通过**

**三条证据，方向一致。**

**其一，这个惯例自带终止条款，而条款是以针对它的套利为触发的。**
McLean 给出的三条解散条件：任一共和国财政赤字超过预算收入的 10%；
任一共和国外汇储备低于一个月进口额；**共和国之间的资本转移超过银行存款总额的 5%**。

**第三条是关键。它写明了：如果足够多的人把钱搬走，这个安排就结束。**
于是搬钱既是理性的，也是自我实现的，**而且是条约本身教给所有人的。**

**其二，市场早就在动，而且动得很大。**
Newstream：**「Za dva týdny od rozdělení Československa odplynulo z ČNB 43 procent devizových rezerv,
vklady na účtech se přesouvaly ze slovenských do českých bank.」**
两周内央行外汇储备流失 43%，存款从斯洛伐克的银行搬到捷克的银行。
Williams 的表述是同一件事的另一半：**「banks, firms and individuals to hoard cash in Czech banks
and convert to foreign currency in anticipation of devaluation of the Slovak crown」**。

**其三，官方自己知道，并且公开否认。**
1993-01-15 捷克财长说「无论如何明天不会有分币」，同日梅恰尔说协议不存在。
而印花在**半年前**已经下单，一亿六千万张，在**波哥大**秘密印刷，
四百箱运到布拉格时国家银行的雇员不知道里面是什么（Novinky 2013）。

**裁定：日期是意外，事件不是。**
**这条前置条件要求的是「没被预期」，而这里被预期得非常充分，
预期还留下了可测的痕迹（储备两周去掉 43%）。所以不通过。**

**⚠ 三条解散条件目前只有 McLean 2012（英国下议院苏格兰事务委员会书面证据）这一个二级出处，
条约原文未取。43% 这个数只有 Newstream 转述，捷克国家银行 1993 年报未取。两项都记账。**

---

### 四、前置条件二（实体连续）：**通过**

跨过 1993-02-08 没有战争，没有封锁，没有产能中断。
**关税同盟与劳动力自由流动是明确要继续的**：McLean 记「the customs union and freedom of
movement of labor were intended to remain in place indefinitely」。
唯一的中断是 02-03 至 02-08 之间的支付冻结与加强的边境现金检查，
Radio Prague：「all payments between the two new countries stopped and border controls were
stepped up to prevent transfers of cash」。**那是对钱的中断，不是对货的中断，正是本臂需要的形状。**

**裁定：通过。**

---

### 五、由此的处置

**子设计乙不作为事件研究上马。**前置条件一是当初为因果识别写的，
它不通过就意味着这里没有干净的「未预期冲击」可用，**硬上就是在一个人人已经站好位的市场里做事件研究。**

**改为描述臂，取它的识别内容。**

---

### 六、这个载体仍然给出的东西

**其一，同一张钞票，同一天，因为它在谁手里、在哪一边被贴花，成为两种货币中的一种，起始平价 1:1。**
五个月后其中一种被一纸行政决定减掉 10%。**两个日期上都没有任何实体发生变化。**

**其二，按人分配又出现了一次，形式比德国 1990 弱。**
15 岁以上每人 4000 克朗可以拿现钞，超出的只能进账户。
**这不是按人给不同的比率，是按人给不同的流动性。**德国那次分的是价值，这次分的是形态。
**两次都说明「谁持有」进入了那个数的定义，而标准构造里没有这个位置。**

**其三，与德国 1990 配成一对，而且是相反的法律处理。**

| | 德国 1990 | 捷克斯洛伐克 1992-93 |
|---|---|---|
| 规则怎么对待针对它的套利 | **宣布无效**（"Umgehungsgeschäfte sind nichtig."） | **写成终止触发条件**（跨共和国资本转移 > 存款 5%） |
| 结果 | 规则活下来，套利被作废 | 套利做到触发，规则死掉 |

**两种处理，一个共同前提：作者都预先承认那个值是可以被行为改变的。**
**你没办法用开户或者转账去改变一个关于世界的事实。你只能改变一条规则的适用。**
**两份法律文件，一份靠宣布规避无效来护住它，一份直接把规避写成它的死亡条件，
都是在承认同一件事。**

**⚠ 本节第三条是论证，不是测量。它不进任何臂的计分。**

---

### 七、给 §4.14 加的载体先验剔除规则

**规则（本块立）：一个惯例如果在自己的条文里写了以套利为触发的终止条款，
它的终结在原理上不可能是意外，因此它永远过不了「意外」这条前置条件。
这类载体应当在查证之前就从事件研究的候选池里剔除，直接进描述臂。**

**为什么值得单立：**避免下一次再花一轮去查一个结构上注定查不出「意外」的载体。
**判别式很便宜：去读那个安排的条文里有没有解散条件，有就出局。**

---

### 八、欠账

| 欠什么 | 状态 |
|---|---|
| 《捷克斯洛伐克货币安排条约》原文，三条解散条件的一级出处 | 未取，本块靠 McLean 2012 |
| 捷克国家银行 1993 年报，两周 43% 储备流失的一级数字 | 未取 |
| 换钞窗口日期冲突：Aktuálně 记 02-04 开始，捷克国家银行行史记 02-08 至 02-12 | 未解，本块采国家银行行史 |
| 未成年人限额冲突：ČT24 记「15 岁以下 1000」，Aktuálně 记「18 岁以下 1000」 | 未解，本块采国家银行行史的「15 岁以上 4000」，未成年额度不写 |
| 1993-07 贬值 10% 的确切日期与斯洛伐克央行文告 | 未取 |

---

### 九、一句话

**这个载体过不了因果识别那条门槛，原因不是数据不够，是那个惯例把自己的死法写在了条文里，
所以没有人可能对它的死感到意外。而它给出的东西正好是这件事本身：
一个各方共用的安排，在它的第一页上就承认了，只要足够多的人不再照它行事，它就不再成立。**

---

## B30-22 第三块：前置条件一被拆成两条，捷克重裁，以及三个更干净的载体

```
AUTHORITY:  对第二块裁定的更正，加载体搜寻。
DATE:       2026-08-31
STATUS:     第二块的「前置条件一不通过」作废，改为「一甲不通过、一乙通过」。
            子设计乙的主载体由捷克斯洛伐克换成俄罗斯 1993-07-24。
```

### 一、第二块错在哪

**第二块写的是「事件被预期，所以前置条件一不通过」。**
**这是把两个量词不同的命题按同一个标准裁的，是失败模式 111 的一个新实例。**

```
命题甲   「惯例要变」被预期            捷克那次：是。储备两周去 43%，条约自带触发条款
命题乙   「惯例变成什么样」被预期      这是另一个命题，第二块根本没测
```

**而本框架的核心主张是关于程序的，不是关于水平的。**
所以能识别它的冲击必须是**程序内容**的冲击。命题甲是水平方向的信息，
**它告诉你钱会变毛，它不告诉你贴花规则、按人限额、五天通知、支付冻结、按territory归属这一整套长什么样。**

**而且那 43% 跑的是另一条边。**
资金往德国马克和美元跑，定价的是 koruna 兑硬通货那条边。
**本臂要测的是跨境相对价格那条边，以及捷克克朗兑斯洛伐克克朗那条边。
在第一条边上站好位，对第二条边一个字都没说。**

**所以前置条件一从今天起拆成两条：**

```
一甲  变动的方向被预期了吗          用于判断能不能在水平上做事件研究
一乙  变动的程序内容被预期了吗      用于判断能不能对程序做事件研究   <- 本框架要的是这条
```

**只有一乙是本臂的门槛。一甲不通过只禁止在被预期的那条边上做读数，不禁止整个载体。**

---

### 二、捷克重裁：一乙通过

**找不到任何一份事前预测了条款的材料。**去搜了捷克语与英语的当时报道与回顾，
**没有一处出现有人在 1993-02-03 之前说出 1:1、四千限额、贴花、五天、支付冻结中的任何一条。**
**⚠ 这个「没找到」的证据力很弱**：回顾报道本来就不报没兑现的预测，
**所以本节不靠它，靠下面三条结构证据。**

**其一，1993-01-15 官方公开否认，而条款那时已经在做。**
捷克财长 Kočárník：「V žádném případě nedojde k měnové odluce zítra」；
梅恰尔同日称协议不存在。而印花 1992 年七月末已下单一亿六千万张。

**其二，印花是在国外秘密印的，而且连自己人都不知道。**
捷克的在波哥大印（Novinky），四百箱运到布拉格时国家银行雇员不知道里面是什么；
斯洛伐克的由 Canadian Banknote Company 加印国徽与文字（斯洛伐克国家银行）。
路透的回顾说是「printed by a British firm to ensure secrecy」，
**三个来源三个供应商，本块只取三者共同的那一句：在国外印，为了保密。**

**其三，也是最硬的一条：条款在水平上是空的。**
分割时两边都按 **1:1**，两种新克朗以平价起步。
**如果市场事前把条款定了价，斯洛伐克克朗不会以平价开出。**
它以平价开出，然后**五个月后**由斯洛伐克央行一纸行政决定减 10%，
此后一直弱于捷克克朗直到 2009 年入欧元（路透）。
**贬值不在分割日，在分割之后的一个独立行政动作上。**
**所以分割那一刻的条款，在水平维度上就是零假设本身。**

**裁定：一甲不通过，一乙通过。**
**操作后果：本载体可用，但读数不许落在 koruna 兑硬通货那条边上，
只许落在跨境相对价格与 CZK/SKK 上。**

---

### 三、去找了更干净的载体，找到三个加一个反例

#### 3.1 俄罗斯 1993-07-24，本设计乙的新主载体

**Conway，普林斯顿 Essays in International Finance 197，原文：**

> "On July 24, 1993, the CBR and the presidency of Russia announced that Soviet and
> Russian banknotes issued between 1961 and 1992 would be taken out of circulation at
> midnight the next day."

> **"Members of the currency area were given no official warning of this move."**

**第二句是本臂要的那句，而且它是关于币区其他成员的，不是关于散户的。**
**一个共享程序的一方，在不通知其他各方的情况下，在一夜之间改掉了那个程序。
这正是「程序变而环境不变」，而且「没有官方预警」是一个学术专著里的直接陈述。**

**条款（俄罗斯央行电报 N 131-93，1993-07-24）：**

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 通知 | 07-24 宣布，次日午夜生效 |
| 兑换窗口 | 07-26 至 08-07 |
| 每人限额 | **35,000 卢布**（约 35 美元），两天后叶利钦令改为 **100,000 卢布** |
| 资格 | 按**国内护照上的登记地** |
| 记录方式 | **兑换额盖在护照上** |
| 事前 | **有传闻，当局否认** |
| 时机 | **在休假季执行，很多人不在自己的登记地** |

**最后两行合起来读：一个人能换多少，由他护照上的登记戳决定；
而这次操作被安排在他大概率不在那个登记地的时候。**
**这不是分配规则加上了执行摩擦，这是分配规则本身把「你在不在场」写进了你能拿到多少。**

**后果，按 Conway：**格鲁吉亚 1993-07 加速退出；土库曼 1993-11；
**哈萨克与乌兹别克 1993-11-15**；亚美尼亚与摩尔多瓦 1993-11。

**前置条件二要逐国筛，而这正是那条规则该干的活：**
格鲁吉亚（内战）、亚美尼亚（纳卡与封锁）、摩尔多瓦（德涅斯特河沿岸）**出局**，
**哈萨克、乌兹别克、土库曼、白俄罗斯留下。**四个单位够跑。

#### 3.2 苏联 1991-01-22，纯程序冲击，零通知

戈尔巴乔夫 1991-01-22 签令，公众 **01-23 从电视上知道**，
兑换窗口 **01-23 至 01-25，三天**，作废 50 与 100 卢布券，
每人不受限地换 **1000 卢布**（约合一个月工资），**银行事先没有被告知**。
计划回收 300 亿，实收 140 亿。

**实体连续完美，程序变动彻底，通知为零。**
**这是子设计甲那一格里目前最干净的一例，虽然它是作废不是面额重整。**

#### 3.3 爱沙尼亚 1992-06-20，突然独立后自建单位

三天现金兑换窗口，存款走另一套程序，脱离卢布区。
**⚠ 条款（每人限额与超限额比率）未取实，UPI 当日报道太薄，
爱沙尼亚银行一级出处未取。列为候选，不进本块任何论证。**

#### 3.4 反例方向：克里米亚 2014，合图而且被提前

**原定卢布与格里夫纳并行到 2016 年，普京 2014 年四月以「乌克兰违约风险」为由提前，
格里夫纳收款截止 2014-06-01。**
**约二十一个月的过渡期被砍成约两个月。**
**这一例的形状特别：变的不是惯例，是那个惯例的换届时间表本身，而且是单方面缩短。**
**⚠ 行政折算率未取。列为候选。**

---

### 四、载体表，按一乙排

| 载体 | 日期 | 通知 | 一乙（程序内容被预期？） | 二（实体连续） | 用途 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **俄罗斯币区** | 1993-07-24 | 约 24 小时 | **否，"no official warning"** | 逐国筛，四国留下 | **子设计乙主载体** |
| 苏联 Pavlov | 1991-01-22 | 零 | **否，银行都没被告知** | 完美 | 子设计甲 |
| 捷克斯洛伐克 | 1993-02-08 | 五天 | **否**（一甲是） | 通过 | 描述臂，可升为读数臂 |
| 爱沙尼亚 | 1992-06-20 | 未查 | 未查 | 未查 | 候选 |
| 克里米亚 | 2014-06-01 | 约两个月 | 未查 | 未查 | 反例候选 |
| 德国 | 1990-07-01 | 约六周 | **是，条款早公布** | 通过 | B32 已收，做恒等式不做事件研究 |

**德国那一行值得注意：条款提前六周公布，所以它一乙不通过，
而 B32 用它做的不是事件研究，是恒等式与套利观测。用法对得上。**

---

### 五、这一块改了什么

1. **前置条件一拆成一甲一乙，只有一乙是门槛。**
2. **第二块「捷克不通过」作废，改为一甲不通过、一乙通过，载体可用但限定读数落在哪条边。**
3. **子设计乙的主载体改为俄罗斯 1993-07-24。**
4. **第二块立的那条载体先验剔除规则保留，但要改口径**：
   条文里有以套利为触发的终止条款，剔除的是**一甲**，不是整个载体。
   **一个自带终止触发的惯例，它的死是可预期的，它的死法不是。**

---

### 六、欠账与冲突

| 项 | 状态 |
|---|---|
| 捷克 1993 事前预测条款的当时材料 | 搜过，未找到。此「未找到」证据力弱，本块不依赖 |
| 印花供应商三说不一：波哥大（Novinky）、Canadian Banknote（斯洛伐克国家银行）、英国厂（路透） | 未解。只取共同项「国外、保密」 |
| 兑换窗口：斯洛伐克国家银行记斯方 02-04 至 02-07，捷克国家银行记捷方 02-08 至 02-12，Aktuálně 记捷方 02-04 起 | **未解，且有可能是两共和国真的不同窗口，不是来源冲突。要一级文件** |
| 俄罗斯 1993 电报 N 131-93 与叶利钦令原文 | 未取，本块靠 Conway 与二级汇编 |
| 爱沙尼亚 1992 条款：`nr 030`、`nr 021`、`nr 026` | **2026-09-05 重判：办完，本行此前过期。** 三份的内容已知且两处独立一致（改革执行日 06-20；账面重估；傀儡交易无效、已得款入国库）|
| 爱沙尼亚 1992 条款：其余四号 | **真欠账，内容未知。** 只知道有这几号，不知道各写了什么。**正确的写法是「内容未知」，不是「正本未取」** |
| 克里米亚 2014 行政折算率 | 未取 |
| 斯洛伐克侧数字：加印 8600 万张 280 亿，实换 7140 万张 269 亿 | 已取，来自斯洛伐克国家银行，未用于本块论证 |

---

### 七、一句话

**「会变」被知道，「变成什么样」没被知道，这两件事分开之后，捷克那一例回到了可用的一侧，
而俄罗斯 1993-07-24 提供了一句更硬的：共享程序的一方在没有通知任何其他成员的情况下，
在一夜之间改掉了那个程序。那正是「程序变而环境不变」这个条件在历史上直接给出的样子。**

---

## B30-22 第四块：俄罗斯 1993-07-24 的一级文件，两份，全文已读

```
AUTHORITY:  §4.14 子设计乙主载体的前置文件补齐。设计件写明「跑之前必须补一级文件」，本块办这件事。
DATE:       2026-08-31
STATUS:     两份原文到手。条款表由原文重建，第三块里那张三级出处的表作废。
```

### 一、文件一：中央银行电报 № 131-93，1993-07-24

标题：**《О ПОРЯДКЕ ИЗЪЯТИЯ ИЗ ОБРАЩЕНИЯ ДЕНЕЖНЫХ БИЛЕТОВ ОБРАЗЦА 1961—1992 ГГ.》**
签署：**Росцентрбанк В. Геращенко，24.07.93 г.，№ 131-93**

#### 1.1 它自己写的理由

> «в целях устранения **множественности модификаций денежных билетов одного достоинства
> в обращении** и в связи с достаточностью в резерве банкнот и монеты Банка России
> образца 1993 года»

**这句要单独拿出来。发文方自己给出的目的是「消除同一面额在流通中存在多种形态」。**
**也就是说：好几个不同的实物，都在声称自己是同一个数，而发文方要求只有一个。**
**这不是一个关于稀缺、成本或需求的理由。这是一个关于「哪一个实物有资格代表那个数」的理由，
而且是由一方单独裁定的。**

#### 1.2 条款，按持有人分档

| 持有人 | 窗口 | 现钞限额 | 超出部分 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **俄联邦公民及同等人员，在其境内有 прописка 或居留许可** | **07-26 至 08-07，两周** | **35,000 卢布** | **进 6 个月定期存款**，计息 |
| **其他国家公民，临时在俄境内** | **仅 07-26 当天** | **15,000 卢布** | 未规定 |
| **组织（法人）** | 07-26 当天交存 | **「Прямой обмен изымаемых денежных билетов на банкноты Банка России образца 1993 года и монету не разрешается.」** 不许直接换现钞，只能进账户 | 且交存额不得超过该组织的库存现金限额加 07-25 日终营业收入 |

原文两句，逐字：

> «Граждане Российской Федерации и приравненные к ним лица, **имеющие прописку или вид
> на жительство на ее территории**, могут в течение двух недель, с 26 июля по 7 августа
> 1993 года, произвести **свободный одноразовый личный обмен** изымаемых денежных билетов
> Госбанка СССР, Сберегательного Банка России **при наличии паспортов**.»

> «Граждане других государств, временно находящиеся на территории Российской Федерации,
> могут произвести в отделениях Сберегательного банка России **в течение 26 июля 1993 года**
> свободный обмен указанных денежных билетов **на сумму до 15,0 тыс. рублей** при
> предъявлении паспорта.»

**同一张钞票，同一天，在俄联邦有登记的自然人手里可换到 35,000 的限额、有两周时间；
在外国公民手里限额 15,000、只有一天；在法人手里根本不能换成现钞。**
**限额比 35:15，窗口比 14 天比 1 天。**

#### 1.3 登记与盖章

> «О проведенном обмене в паспортах (для военнослужащих — в военном билете, удостоверении
> личности) **проставляется отметка учреждения Сбербанка России и печать**.»

**资格由护照上的 прописка 决定，行使过一次之后盖章注销。**
**「одноразовый」这个词与德国 1990 附件一第六条那句
「Der Antrag kann nur einmalig bei einem Geldinstitut gestellt werden.」是同一件装置。**

#### 1.4 两处时间上的东西，值得单记

**其一，追溯的资格日。**

> «Предприятия, организации и учреждения, имеющие **по состоянию на 16 июля 1993 года**
> в своих кассах изымаемую из оборота денежную наличность должны заактировать ее на начало
> дня 26 июля…»

**资格日是 07-16，比公开电报早八天。规则伸回到它被宣布之前的一个日期上。**

**其二，内部指令早于公开电报两天。**电报正文自己引用：

> «Указания о порядке работы учреждений Сберегательного банка по обмену гражданам изымаемой
> из обращения денежной наличности изложены в **телеграмме Банка России от 22 июля 1993 года
> № 397**.»

**07-22 已经有给储蓄银行的操作指令，07-24 才有公开电报。**

---

### 二、文件二：总统令 № 1107，1993-07-26

标题：**《Об обеспечении нормального функционирования денежной системы Российской Федерации》**
签署：**Президент Российской Федерации Б. Ельцин，Москва, Кремль，26 июля 1993 года，№ 1107**

> «В целях защиты прав и законных интересов граждан Российской Федерации, **во изменение
> ранее принятых решений**, а также обеспечения нормального функционирования денежной системы
> Российской Федерации постановляю:»

条款：

- **限额由 35,000 提到 100,000**：「увеличить лимит свободного одноразового обмена … **на сумму
  до 100 тыс. рублей на каждого человека**」，资格仍是「граждан Российской Федерации и лиц,
  **имеющих прописку или вид на жительство на ее территории**」；
- 且**不计入** 1992 年版一万卢布券的自由兑换（«без учета свободного обмена банкнот Банка России
  образца 1992 года достоинством 10 тыс. рублей»）；
- 窗口延到**整个八月**：「обмен производить в течение августа 1993 года」；
- 保留 1、3、5、10 卢布券继续流通；
- **最后一条最要紧**：「**в ходе проведения обменных операций принимать оперативно решения
  по корректировке порядка обмена**, направленные на обеспечение гарантий социальной защиты
  граждан Российской Федерации.」
- 「Настоящий Указ ввести в действие **с момента подписания**.」

**最后一条是一份一级文件里的直接授权：在兑换正在进行的过程中，随时修改兑换办法。**
**加上「во изменение ранее принятых решений」和两天内限额乘以 2.86、窗口从 8 月 7 日延到 8 月底，
这份文件在原文里说明了：那套条款不由任何外部的东西钉住，它由发文方在执行中调。**

---

### 三、三例并置

**三份法律文件，三种对付「针对规则的套利」的技术，一个共同前提。**

| | 德国 1990-05-18 条约附件一第六条 | 捷克斯洛伐克 1992 货币安排条约 | 俄罗斯 1993-07-24 电报 131-93 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 按持有人分档 | **比率**：1:1 / 2:1 / 3:1，按出生年、居民身份、法人 | **流动性形态**：15 岁以上 4000 现钞，其余进账户 | **限额与窗口**：35,000／两周 对 15,000／一天 对 法人不得换现钞 |
| 一次性条款 | 「Der Antrag kann nur einmalig bei einem Geldinstitut gestellt werden.」 | 每人一次 | 「свободный **одноразовый** личный обмен」，护照盖章 |
| 对付规避的技术 | **宣布规避交易无效**（「Umgehungsgeschäfte sind nichtig.」） | **把规避写成终止触发条件**（跨共和国资本转移 > 存款 5%） | **把资格日追溯到宣布之前**（07-16），**并保留在执行中改办法的权力** |
| 结果 | 规则活下来，套利被作废 | 套利做到触发，规则死掉 | 规则活下来，两天后被发文方自己改宽 |

**共同前提：三份文件的作者都预先承认，那个值可以被持有人的行为改变。**
**你不能用开户、转账、或者换个人去排队来改变一个关于世界的事实。你只能改变一条规则对你的适用。**
**这一条在第二块里是论证，现在有三份一级文件各自用不同的技术承认它，仍然是论证，但载重变了。**

---

### 四、前置条件的最终状态

**一乙（程序内容被预期？）：不通过被预期，即通过本臂门槛。三条证据来源不同、对象不同：**

- **对币区其他成员**：Conway，「Members of the currency area were given no official warning of this move.」
- **对国内持有人**：РИА，「Власти подливали масла в огонь, **регулярно опровергая эти слухи**」，
  且电报发在 **07-24 星期六**，储蓄银行不营业、很多人在休假或在别墅。
- **对条款本身**：条款在 07-26 存在，07-26 当天就被总统令改掉。**发文方自己两天前都不知道最终条款。**

**第三条最强，而且它是一个新形状的证据：如果条款连发文方在两天后都要改，
那么任何外部方在事前都不可能知道它。**

**二（实体连续）：逐国筛，未办。**格鲁吉亚（内战）、亚美尼亚（纳卡与封锁）、
摩尔多瓦（德涅斯特河沿岸）预计出局；哈萨克、乌兹别克、土库曼、白俄罗斯预计留下。
**「预计」两个字要换成查证，这是下一块的活。**

---

### 五、第三块里作废的东西

第三块那张俄罗斯条款表来自三级出处，**现在按原文重建，两处要改：**

| 项 | 第三块写的 | 原文 |
|---|---|---|
| 超出限额的处置 | 未写 | **进 6 个月定期存款，计息**，不是没收 |
| 外国公民 | 未写 | **限额 15,000，窗口仅 07-26 一天** |
| 法人 | 未写 | **不许换现钞，且交存额不得超过库存现金限额加前一日营业收入** |
| 总统令延期 | 未写 | 窗口由 08-07 延到**八月底** |

**第三块表里「每人 35,000，两天后改为 100,000，按护照登记地，盖章，事前否认，休假季」六项，
逐条与原文相符，不改。**

### 六、欠账

| 项 | 状态 |
|---|---|
| 电报 131-93 的转录出处 | 维基文库，注明源自《Коммерсантъ》／《Власть》1993-07-26 第 29 期。**是当时刊物的转录，不是俄央行档案原件** |
| 总统令 1107 | **kremlin.ru 官方全文，注 pravo.gov.ru。这一份是原件级** |
| 电报 № 397（1993-07-22，给储蓄银行的操作指令） | **2026-09-05 重判：不欠，本行此前过期。** 它由 `162-93` 点名，位置定死；**而承重的条款（限额、窗口、超额去向）在 `131-93` 正本里，已全文在手。** 「唯一支点」这个说法同日撤回：那条读数由 `162-93` 与 `131-93` 两份一级件各自撑着 |

---

### 七、一句话

**一份一级文件写着，它的目的是消除同一面额在流通中的多种形态；
它按持有人的护照登记地给出三档不同的限额与窗口；它把资格日追溯到自己被宣布之前；
两天后，签发它的政权把限额乘以 2.86 并授权在执行过程中继续改它。
「那个数由一套程序产出，而程序由维护它的一方在调」这句话，在这份文件里不需要推论，
它是逐条写着的。**

---

## B30-22 第五块：逐国筛，以及哈萨克把同一件工具原样用了一遍

```
AUTHORITY:  §4.14 子设计乙前置条件二的逐国查证，加一个未预期到的发现。
DATE:       2026-08-31
STATUS:     筛出四个可用单位加两个先行退出的对照。哈萨克 1993-11-12 一级文件全文（前三条）已读。
            前置条件二判为「暂定通过」，需要一份冲突数据集坐实，未取。
```

### 一、先补两条第四块没有的事实，两条都改变读法

**其一，新卢布券在 1993 年初就印好了，而且没有发给其他各国。**
Encyclopedia of Russian History「Ruble Zone」条：

> "It began to print new Russian ruble notes...in early 1993, but **did not send these new
> rubles to the other states**."

**七月那一下不是一个突然的决定落在一个没准备的世界上。
是印好了、扣住了、然后在一个星期六把旧的作废。**
**第四块讲的「资格日追溯到 07-16」是八天，这一条是几个月。**

**其二，留下来的条件是什么，有人写下来了。**
Goldberg、Ickes 与 Ryterman，*Departures from the Ruble Zone*（1994）：

> "The currency reform/confiscation of July 1993, which required pre-1993 rubles to be
> exchanged for new rubles in limited quantities, **forced the hand of the other remaining
> ruble zone countries**."

而俄方给出的留下选项，同文：**"complete subordination of monetary policy"**。

**所以这不是单纯的驱逐，是一次单方面重新指定共享程序：
要么接受由一方独家书写的新程序，要么离开这张图。**
**这一句正好是本框架核心主张的操作化版本，而且是别人在 1994 年写的，不是本项目造的。**

---

（Conway 说引入自己的货币后又谈判重新加入）。**用到白俄罗斯之前必须查实。**

### 三、前置条件二：逐国筛

**判据（§4.14 原文）：跨过那个日期，产能、库存、运输没有同时被战争或封锁打断。**

| 国 | 退出日期 | 当时状态 | 判 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 格鲁吉亚 | 1993-07 | 阿布哈兹战争进行中，苏呼米 1993-09-27 陷落；同期明格列利亚内战 | **出局** |
| 亚美尼亚 | 1993-11 | 纳卡战争至 1994-05 停火；阿塞拜疆与土耳其封锁 | **出局** |
| 阿塞拜疆 | 1993-07 | 同上，战争一方 | **出局** |
| 塔吉克 | 1995-05 | 内战 1992 至 1997 | **出局**，且日期离冲击太远 |
| 摩尔多瓦 | 1993-11 | 德涅斯特河沿岸 1992-07 停火，退出时无交火，但领土分割且左岸另行流通 | **存疑，单列** |
| **哈萨克** | **1993-11-15** | 无武装冲突 | **留下** |
| **乌兹别克** | **1993-11-15** | 无武装冲突 | **留下** |
| **土库曼** | **1993-11** | 无武装冲突 | **留下** |
| **白俄罗斯** | **1993-11** | 无武装冲突 | **留下，但退出状态待查** |
| 吉尔吉斯 | 1993-05 | 无武装冲突，**先于冲击退出** | **对照组** |
| 乌克兰 | 1992-01 起 | 无武装冲突，**远先于冲击** | **对照组** |

**四个处理单位，两个先行退出的对照。够跑。**

**⚠ 本表的战争判定来自通行的冲突年表，没有过一份冲突数据集（UCDP／PRIO 之类）。
前置条件二判为「暂定通过」，正式跑之前要用数据集把「退出日期所在的季度有没有战事」逐国核一遍。
摩尔多瓦那一行尤其要单独裁。**

---

### 四、意外的发现：哈萨克把同一件工具原样用了一遍，而且加重了一档

**被赶出去的那一方，四个月后对自己的居民用了同一套装置。**

一级文件：**《Постановление Государственной комиссии Республики Казахстан по введению
национальной валюты от 12 ноября 1993 года № 1 «О порядке и условиях введения национальной
валюты Республики Казахстан»》**，前三条全文已读（第四条被付费墙截断，只读到开头）。

**第 2 条，折算率：**

> «Банкноты образца 1961 — 1992 гг., находящиеся в обращении, рублевые счета, вклады и
> обязательства на территории Республики Казахстан, обмениваются на тенге **по обменному
> курсу 1 тенге за 500 рублей**.»

**第 3 条，自然人：**

> «**Физическое лицо старше 16 лет, являющееся резидентом Республики Казахстан**, может
> произвести обмен банкнот образца 1961 — 1992 гг. в размере **до 100 тысяч рублей** на
> наличные тенге … в период с 08.00 часов 15 ноября 1993 г. **до 18.00 часов 20 ноября 1993 г.**»

> «Данный обмен производится **только один раз**, и факт его проведения фиксируется
> **в разделе «Особые отметки» паспорта** или документа, заменяющего паспорт.»

**超限额部分，这一段是本块最要紧的：**

> «Средства, превышающие указанный лимит, зачисляются в тенге … на **специальный лицевой
> счет в банке без права использования на срок до 6 месяцев**. В последующем **источники и
> законность происхождения этих сверхлимитных средств рассматриваются специальной комиссией**.
> … **По решению комиссий указанные средства могут разблокироваться** для их использования
> физическими лицами.»

**超过那条线的钱，冻六个月，然后由一个专门委员会审查这笔钱的来源与合法性，
再由委员会决定要不要解冻。**

**这比德国 1990 和俄罗斯 1993-07 都更进一步。**
德国按出生年与住址给不同的比率；俄罗斯给不同的限额、窗口与形态；
**哈萨克在超限额那一段上，让那笔钱算不算钱这件事本身成为一个逐案的行政裁量。**
**同一张钞票，在同一个人手里，在同一个日期，值多少，取决于一个委员会事后怎么看他。**

**第 4 条，法人：**窗口是 **11-15 当天 08:00 至 14:00，六个小时**，
对自然人是**五天**，而且只能进账户不能拿现钞。（限额部分被付费墙截断，未读。）

**三例的装置对照，加哈萨克一列：**

| | 德国 1990 | 俄罗斯 1993-07 | **哈萨克 1993-11** |
|---|---|---|---|
| 按持有人分的是 | 比率 1:1／2:1／3:1 | 限额 35,000／15,000／法人零，窗口 14 天／1 天 | **限额 100,000，窗口 5 天／6 小时** |
| 一次性装置 | 「nur einmalig bei einem Geldinstitut」 | 「одноразовый」＋护照盖章 | 「только один раз」＋**护照「Особые отметки」栏** |
| 超限额处置 | 换更差的比率 | 6 个月定期存款，计息 | **6 个月冻结，然后委员会审来源与合法性，逐案决定解不解冻** |
| 年龄／居民条件 | 出生年分三档 | прописка 或居留许可 | **16 岁以上且为居民** |

**四个不同的政权，三年之内，四份互不引用的法律文件，同一件工具。**
**这不是本项目挑出来的相似，是把「按持有人给同一张纸不同的值」这个动作写下来时，
可用的写法本来就那么几种。**

---

### 五、一个顺手的对照，说明「有没有事先协商」是可见的

哈萨克那份文件的关联文件列表里有两份 1993-05-20 的双边文件：
**《Соглашение об организации расчетов между Национальным банком Кыргызской Республики
и Национальным банком Республики Казахстан в связи с введением Кыргызской Республикой
национальной валюты》**，以及同日的《Соглашение о денежно-кредитных отношениях…》。

**吉尔吉斯 1993-05 那次退出，留下了事前签好的双边结算安排。
俄罗斯 1993-07 那次，Conway 的原文是「no official warning」，没有对应的东西。**
**「协商过的程序变更」和「单方面的程序变更」在档案里留下的痕迹不一样，而且这个痕迹可查。**
**这给一乙提供了一个可复用的判别式：去看有没有事前日期的双边文件。有就是协商，没有就往单方面上判。**

---
